## Direction: FOUNDATION-FIRST (bottom-up), 1:1 Java

There is NO object hierarchy in C#: `Player` is a flat `sealed class`; `VisibleObject`/`Creature`/`Npc`/`World`/`WorldMap` are MISSING. The C# model is a flat parallel collection built for the packet path. Build the spine bottom-up so the faked feature clusters can later collapse.

**Spine sequence** (each a faithful 1:1 port; Java line counts in parens):
- **F1 `AionObject`** (79) — **DONE** (`Model/GameObjects/AionObject.cs`).
- **F2 `VisibleObject`** (256) + `VisibleObjectController` (124).
- **F3 `Creature`** (522) + `CreatureController` (568) — incl. the TaskId task system.
- **F4 reparent flat `Player` → `Creature`** (Java 1655; C# 1442, **329 referencing files**) — high-risk integration.
- **F5 `Npc`** (393) + `NpcController` (340).
- **F6 `KnownList`**; **F7 `World`/`WorldMap`/`MapRegion`**.

**How to choose the next unit:** never start a node with an unmet dependency. From the in-scope spine cone, pick a unit whose deps all already exist in C#; if none, port the deepest still-missing dep. Read the Java fully, port 1:1, build, commit (code + HANDOFF). **Never stub/defer.** Don't port dependency-free files unrelated to the spine cone.

## Strategy resolved (user, 2026-06-07): big-bang + SCC-leaves-first

- **Doctrine rule 8** (in `Port-Fidelity-Remediation-Plan.md` §3 + memory): when a new faithful port conflicts with existing C# code, **default to 1:1 Java parity and replace the existing code**; only when the conflict is a genuine **C#-vs-Java foundational language difference** take the closest-to-1:1 path instead.
- **Execution model:** `VisibleObject ↔ Creature ↔ World/MapRegion/WorldMapInstance/WorldPosition ↔ KnownList ↔ subsystems` form one big SCC. In a single assembly a partial SCC won't compile, so the SCC core lands as **one final big-bang commit**. Until then, port the SCC's **dependency-free leaves** bottom-up (green each batch). The `WorldPosition` struct→class swap (below) is part of that closing big-bang.

## ⭐ BIG-BANG IN PROGRESS — branch `feature/object-spine-bigbang`

Executing the SCC on a branch via the **compiler-error-frontier** method: port faithful (pure-Java) files in dependency order; the branch build stays **red until the SCC converges**, then merge to main only when green + guardrail + golden pass. **No transitional fakery** — since nobody else is blocked and we merge only when faithful+green, `instanceId` derives from `MapRegion` per Java (the old stored-InstanceId struct was a non-Java artifact). `main` stays green; do NOT merge until convergence.

**CONVERGENCE = FULL LITERAL PORT, NO GAPS (user, 2026-06-08):** port the entire transitive cone faithfully — AI/stats/effect/skill/combat engines AND **de-god-class the packet layer** (`PacketSendUtility` + the ~200 `SM_`/`CM_` packets out of `GameServerConnection` into faithful packet classes). No method-body stubs. This effectively completes the bulk of the server port; grind bottom-up. (Tier-B substrate is almost entirely MISSING in C# — confirmed: `PacketSendUtility`/`AttackResult`/`Effect`/`Item`/`Skill`/`StatFunctions`/`Summon`/stats-containers all absent.)

**✅ geoEngine + Commons.Nio SUBSTRATE COMPLETE (2026-06-08, query path):** ported the entire geoEngine query stack so the Creature SCC's geo deps resolve — math (`Vector2f`/`Vector3f`/`FastMath`/`Matrix3f`/`Ray`), collision (`Collidable`/`CollisionResult(s)`/`CollisionIntention`/`IgnoreProperties`/`UnsupportedCollisionException`), `bih` (`BIHNode`/`BIHTree`), bounding (`BoundingVolume`/`BoundingBox`), scene (`Spatial`/`Node`/`Geometry`/`Mesh`/`DespawnableNode`/`CollisionData`/`mesh` index arrays), models (`GeoMap`/`Terrain`), `utils/TempVars`, `world/geo/GeoService` (Java singleton → `GetInstance()`), AND a faithful `Commons.Nio` (`ByteOrder`/`Buffer`/`Byte/Float/ShortBuffer`+Heap+AsFloat/AsShort views). **`GeoService.GetInstance().CanSee/getZ` now exist** (resolves AggroList + the spine's geo calls). **TWO geoEngine items DEFERRED:** (1) `GeoWorldLoader`(285L) — the `.geo`/PNG **data loader**; terminal leaf (only `GeoService.Init→Load`), **blocked on an external image-library decision** (decodes 16-bit/8-bit terrain PNGs via `ImageIO`/`BufferedImage`/`DataBufferUShort`/`DataBufferByte` — pick System.Drawing vs ImageSharp vs SkiaSharp) + mmap→`File.ReadAllBytes`+`ByteBuffer.Wrap` + parallelStream→PLINQ; (2) `Matrix4f`(1829L) — last math gap, only `BoundingBox.Transform` uses `MultProj`/`ToRotationMatrix`/`SetRotationMatrix`/`SetTranslation`. Neither blocks the SCC compile (both are forward-ref leaves). **Loop now PIVOTS back to the Creature SCC** (engines + spine + packets).

- stats engine: `Stat2`(+`AdditionStat`/`ReverseStat`), `StatCondition`, `NpcStatCalculation`, StatFunction cone (`IStatFunction`,`StatFunction`,`StatAdd/Rate/Sub/Set/Abs`,`StatFunctionProxy`).

**Porting conventions (apply consistently):** clean-candidate scan finds batches but MISSES same-package `extends`/member usage — always verify. XML data classes use **public properties** (C# XmlSerializer needs public; Java `@XmlAccessorType(FIELD)`). `afterUnmarshal`→`AfterUnmarshal()` method. Java `getType()`→`GetTypeValue()` (an extension `GetType()` is shadowed by `Object.GetType()`); other `getType()`→property. Java annotations→C# attributes.

**Deferred (blocked — port with their prereq):** `StatArmor/Weapon/ShieldMasteryFunction`+`PlayerStatFunctions`+`StatCapUtil` (need Creature-`Player`/`Item`/`ItemTemplate`); `Conditions` cone (~25 subclasses → `Effect`/`Skill`); `MotionTime` (Player members); effect subclasses + `Effects` wrapper (→`EffectTemplate`); `SkillLearnTemplate` (→`SkillTemplate`). Out-of-cone (skip): `PlumStatEnum`, `Announcement`, `Letter`.

**✅ LEAF-HARVEST PHASE COMPLETE (~67 files).** Verified by scan: every remaining cone file needs the indivisible core. Specifically the condition cone (all ~25 subclasses) needs `Skill`+`Effect`+`Player`+`Item`+`PacketSendUtility`; all Tier-B containers (`AbstractAI`/`CreatureGameStats`/`CreatureLifeStats`/`EffectController`/`CreatureMoveController`/`ObserveController`/`AggroList`/`TransformModel`) need `Creature` (+packets); and `Creature` needs those containers as field types → one mutual SCC.

**⛔ REMAINING = the indivisible core convergence (NOT a leaf loop).** Must be written as one concentrated multi-file push and compiled together (red until it closes):
`Effect`(1232L)+`EffectTemplate`(572L)+~170 effect subclasses+`Effects` wrapper; `Skill`(1023L)+`SkillTemplate`(409L)+action/condition/property/periodic sub-cones; `Creature`(522L)+`CreatureController`+Tier-B containers; `Npc`/`Pet`/`StaticObject`/`StaticDoor`; spatial container `World`/`WorldMap`/`WorldMapInstance`/`MapRegion`; `PacketSendUtility` (substrate exists: 183 `Sm*`+`GameServerPacket`); reparent flat `Player`→`Creature` (F4, 329 files) + connection send path; reconcile the 4 `WorldPosition` InstanceId sites. Then build green → merge.
**Recommendation:** tackle as a dedicated focused push (hold the Creature/Effect/Skill APIs in-context, port in dependency tiers, build to shrink the frontier) — not 60s loop nibbles, since nothing here is independently verifiable.

**⛰️ PLAYER-REPARENT SURVEY + STAGED PLAN (the convergence inflection point):**
- **Finding:** existing C# `Player.cs` = **wholly divergent `sealed class Player`(1442L)**, NOT extends Creature, shares **ZERO** API with faithful Java Player (every faithful method the SCC calls — GetEquipment/GetEffectController/GetChainSkills/GetInventory/GetPlayerClass/IsDueling/GetStore/GetController/GetGameStats/GetLifeStats/GetAi/GetSkillList/GetCommonData/GetAbyssRank/SetHitTimeBoost — MISSING). The SCC is therefore **already red on faithful-Player-API** (expected). Java Player(1655L extends Creature) has **~40 field-subsystem cones** (Storage/Cooldowns/Equipment/Account/AbsoluteStatOwner/TitleList/Summon/RecipeList/QuestStateList/PrivateStore/PlayerSkillList/PlayerSettings/PlayerGroup/PlayerAllianceGroup/PetList/Pet/PanesterraFaction/NpcFactions/PortalCooldownList/PlayerAccountData/…). **370 C# files reference `Player`** — the divergent ecosystem.
- **Decision (rule 8):** REPLACE divergent Player with faithful `Player : Creature`. Divergent-API callers (largely slop services/packets) go red → replaced/migrated later; this is the same replace-divergent pattern used for SmAttackStatus etc. Class-name collision means faithful cone subsystems (Equipment/Storage/CommonData/…) must replace their divergent namesakes too (can't coexist).
- **Staged approach (bottom-up, like the SCC):** (1) port the MISSING/faithful Player-cone leaves first where they don't collide; (2) replace divergent cone subsystems with faithful 1:1 as needed; (3) port faithful `Player : Creature`(1655L) LAST once its cone is ready; (4) reparent `Summon`/`Pet`/`Trap`/`Homing`/`SiegeNpc`/`StaticObject` (Creature/Npc subclasses); (5) PlayerAI/SummonAI/etc; (6) `AIEngine` reflection-infra; (7) Properties/Actions/Effects-wrapper cones; (8) spatial `World`/`WorldMap*`/`MapRegion`/zones; (9) packets (SM_* cone), DataManager singleton-bridge statics; → green → merge.
- **⚠️ SCALE NOTE:** turning the branch green from here ≈ re-porting the player half of the server (hundreds of files) — the largest remaining phase by far; proceed bottom-up, one faithful unit per tick, red-by-design, guardrail green each commit.
**NEXT concrete action:** begin the Player cone with a MISSING faithful leaf (e.g. `PlayerController`(754L, CreatureController<Player>) or a small player-subsystem), continuing bottom-up toward the faithful `Player` class.

Do this during the convergence/green phase (Tier 4), not piecemeal. DecomposeAction/RideAction follow the `Aion.Commons.Utils.Rnd` convention for consistency.

(`AggroList`/`ObserveController`/`CreatureMoveController`/`TransformModel`/`KnownList` already ported as WIP.)

**CORE PUSH STARTED (F3 spine tier):** ported `Utils/AtomicBoolean` (JDK equiv), `CreatureMoveController`, `TransformModel` (faithful WIP).
**KEY FINDING — core + F4 are ONE convergence (not sequential):** spine classes branch on `instanceof Player`/`instanceof Npc` and call container/`Effect`/`Skill` APIs, so they can't compile until (a) flat `Player` is reparented `: Creature` (F4) and (b) the packet layer gets **Creature/Npc-based ctors** — the existing 183 `Sm*` were built for flat `Player`/`WorldNpc` (e.g. `SmMove`/`SmTransform`/`SmCustomSettings` take `Player`/`WorldNpc`, not Java's `SM_X(Creature)`), and there's no `PacketSendUtility`. So convergence = **port Effect/Skill/Creature/containers/Npc + reparent Player + reconcile the packet ctors + add PacketSendUtility, all together, then build green.** Scope ≈ most of the remaining server (Effect/Skill transitively pull item/npc-template/spawn/geo/ai/skill engines + services + dozens of packets) — multi-session.

**REVISED packet-layer finding (2026-06-08): substrate ALREADY EXISTS.** Earlier "de-god-class ~200 packets" was WRONG — C# already has **183 standalone `Sm*` packet classes** + the `GameServerPacket` abstract base (`Network/Aion/ServerPackets/`, `Network/Aion/GameServerPacket.cs`). The only missing piece is **`PacketSendUtility`** (broadcast helpers). Its blockers: `Legion` (model/team/legion — MISSING), and a **`Player`→connection send path** (C# sends async via `GameServerConnection.SendPacketAsync`, NOT Java's `player.getClientConnection().sendPacket(packet)`; flat `Player` exposes `IsOnline` but no send method). Both are **F4 integration** (Player reparent + connection wiring). So `PacketSendUtility` lands around F4; the engines that call it are likewise gated there. Engines can otherwise reference the existing `Sm*` classes directly.
**After packets:** Effect engine (`Effect`/`EffectTemplate`+subclasses/`EffectController`), Skill engine (`Skill`/`SkillTemplate`+actions/conditions), AI engine (`AbstractAI`/`AIEngine`), stats containers (`CreatureGameStats`/`CreatureLifeStats`), move/observe/transform → then `Creature`+`CreatureController` → `Npc`/`Pet`/`Static*` → spatial container `World`/`WorldMap`/`WorldMapInstance`/`MapRegion` → reparent `Player` (F4) → green → merge.
**Current error frontier:** `Creature` (24 — now the central blocker), `MapRegion` (8), `WorldMapInstance` (4), `WorldMap` (2), `Npc` (2), `GeoService` (2), + `RespawnService`, faithful-`World` (flat-World swap pending).
**CRITICAL PATH = `Creature` + Tier-B cone** (the dominant remaining bulk): `Creature` fields force `AbstractAI`+`AIEngine`, `CreatureGameStats`/`CreatureLifeStats`(+`Stat2`/stat-fns), `EffectController`(+`Effect`/`EffectTemplate`), `CreatureMoveController`, `ObserveController`(+observers), `AggroList`(+`AggroInfo`), `TransformModel`, `Skill`/`SkillTemplate`, `CreatureController`. Then `Npc`/`Pet`/`StaticObject`/`StaticDoor`; spatial container `World`/`WorldMap`/`WorldMapInstance`(+2D/3D/factory)/`MapRegion` (+`ThreadPoolManager` singleton, `ZoneService`/`QuestEngine`/`InstanceEngine`, `PlayerContainer`/`SiegeNpc`/`ShieldService`); reparent `Player` (F4); reconcile InstanceId sites → green → merge.
**⚠ flat-`World` conflict:** the faithful `World` singleton (Java) must REPLACE the existing flat `World/World.cs` (different design) — `ZoneInstance`/`VisibleObject` etc. call `World.GetInstance().GetWorldMap(id)`. Measure flat-World consumers before swapping.
**Backlog (data-compat, like Race):** `ZoneAttributes` is PascalCase with `@XmlEnumValue` tokens (FLY/PVP/DUEL_SAME_RACE/…) only in comments — verify worldmaps XML `flags` parsing actually works; likely needs `[XmlEnum("FLY")]`/SCREAMING_SNAKE re-port for data parity.
**Convergence reconciliation (will resurface once WorldPosition resolves):** 4 legacy sites set the old stored InstanceId — `GameServerConnection.cs:14718/14722`, `InstanceRuntimeService.cs:147` (`with { InstanceId = … }`), `PlayerTeleportService.cs:159` (6-arg ctor). Rework to set position via the spawn/instance flow (derived).
**Spine-wide design decisions (resolved, Java-guided — apply consistently):**
1. **flat-`World` replacement:** swap the 133-line flat `World/World.cs` (packet-path API: `TryAddObject(object)`, `GetNpcs()→IWorldNpcObject`, houses) for the faithful Java `World` singleton (350L: `storeObject(VisibleObject)`, `getWorldMap(int)→WorldMap`, `findVisibleObject`, `updatePosition`, spawn/despawn). Rewrite its ~4 packet-path consumers to the Java API (they're slated to collapse anyway). Pulls in `PlayerContainer`/`SiegeNpc`/`ShieldService`/`AuditLogger`/`BindPointPosition` (port or track).
2. **Controller generics (Java `VisibleObjectController<? extends VisibleObject>` wildcard fields):** C# has no wildcards → use a **non-generic abstract base** `VisibleObjectController` (holds `VisibleObject Owner`, virtual `See/NotSee/OnDelete/...`) + **generic** `VisibleObjectController<T> : VisibleObjectController where T : VisibleObject` (typed `GetOwner():T`/`SetOwner(T)`). Same shape for `CreatureController`/`NpcController`. `VisibleObject` stores the non-generic base; `Creature.GetController()` returns the typed one via `new`. (Rule 8 closest-to-1:1.)
3. **`ThreadPoolManager` (Java static `getInstance()` vs C# DI):** add a Java-faithful static `GetInstance()` + `Execute(Action)` + `Schedule(Action, TimeSpan)` to the existing C# `ThreadPoolManager` (singleton assigned at startup), coexisting with the DI instance the packet path uses.
4. **`AionObject(objId, autoReleaseObjectId)`:** C# base ctor is `AionObject(int)` (the GC auto-release branch is FR-1, deferred). `VisibleObject`'s `super(objId, autoReleaseObjectId)` → C# `base(objId)`; the autoRelease flag wires in at FR-1.

**Build order (bottom-up):** WorldPosition✅ → MapRegion → WorldMapInstance(+2D/3D+factory) → WorldMap → ZoneInstance(+ZoneHandler/AdvancedZoneHandler) → VisibleObjectController → VisibleObject → KnownList/KnownObject → InstanceHandler/GeneralInstanceHandler → GeneralTeam → Creature(+CreatureController) + Tier-B subsystem containers → Npc/NpcController/Pet/StaticObject/StaticDoor → World singleton (+PlayerContainer/SiegeNpc/services) → reparent Player (F4) → reconcile the 4 InstanceId sites + legacy WorldPosition readers → green → merge.

## ⭐ BIG-BANG SCOPE (tiers — full membership)

The object-model spine is one strongly-connected component: in a single C# assembly a partial SCC won't compile, so it lands as **one large red-until-green effort** (recommend a dedicated branch/worktree). Build it bottom-up internally; commit only when the whole assembly builds + guardrail passes. Validate against golden packet/formula fixtures where they exist.

**Tier A — core spine (~3.7k Java L, ~20 files):** `WorldPosition`(class,224) · `MapRegion`(223) · `WorldMap`(215) · `WorldMapInstance`(349)+`WorldMap2D/3DInstance`+`WorldMapInstanceFactory` · `World`(350) · `VisibleObject`(256)+`VisibleObjectController`(124) · `Creature`(522)+`CreatureController`(568) · `Npc`(393)+`NpcController`(340) · `Pet`(49) · `StaticObject`(18)+`StaticDoor`(87) · `KnownList`(269)+`KnownObject`(51) · `ZoneInstance`(177)+`ZoneHandler`/`AdvancedZoneHandler` · `InstanceHandler`+`GeneralInstanceHandler` · `GeneralTeam` · support: `RespawnService`/`GeoService`/`ThreadPoolManager`/`ZoneService`/`QuestEngine` (minimal), `PositionUtil` game-object overloads.

**Tier B — Creature field subsystem cones (~5.5k+ Java L *before* their own sub-cones; the real bulk):** `AbstractAI`(465)+`AIEngine`(151) · `CreatureGameStats`(396)+`CreatureLifeStats`(377)+`Stat2`(113)+stat-functions/modifiers · `EffectController`(778)+`EffectTemplate`(572)+`Effect`+effect-subclass cone · `Skill`(1023)+`SkillTemplate`(409)+skill action/condition/property cone · `CreatureMoveController`(103) · `ObserveController`(270)+observer cone · `AggroList`(216)+`AggroInfo`(64) · `TransformModel`(220). Each drags in further subclasses — Tier B is the dominant cost and should be sized again before starting.

**Tier C — F4 Player reparent (highest risk):** flat C# `Player` (~1442L, sealed, own `ObjectId`/`Name`/`Position`) → `Creature`. Java `Player` 1655L. **329 referencing files.** Reconcile inherited vs flat fields.

**WorldPosition migration (part of Tier A):** struct→class touches **64 files / ~273 `.WorldId` + ~101 `.InstanceId` + ~72 `.Heading` + `with{InstanceId=…}` sites**; behavior-affecting (value→ref, `with`→setters, **stored→derived InstanceId** via `mapRegion.getParent()`).

**Open scoping question (decide before starting):** can the new spine land *parallel* (new abstract types + Tier B containers) while flat `Player`/`WorldNpc` keep working, deferring Tier C (F4) — OR must `WorldPosition` struct→class (used by both new `VisibleObject` and legacy flat code) force F4 simultaneously? The single `WorldPosition` type is the coupling point. Resolve this first; it determines whether the big-bang is "spine+subsystems" (Tier A+B) with F4 as a follow-on, or all three tiers at once.

**Item/npc cone nodes blocked on the SCC core (port during/after big-bang):** `ResultedItem`→`ResultedItemsCollection`/`ExtractedItemsCollection`/`ReturnLocList`/`MultiReturnItem`/`DecomposableItemInfo` (need `Player`+`DataManager`); `Stigma` (`SkillTemplate`); `ItemUseLimits` (`AbyssRankEnum`→`Player`); `ItemActions` (action hierarchy→`Player`/`Item`); `ModifiersTemplate`/`StatFunction` (→`Stat2`); `ItemSetTemplate` (`ItemPart`/`PartBonus`/`FullBonus`); then `ItemTemplate` itself → unblocks `NpcEquippedGear` → `Npc`. `CustomConfig` (port like `WorldConfig`). `AbyssRankEnum` (needs `Player`+`RankingConfig`+`ChatUtil`).

## Blockers / risks

- **✅ substrate progress (2026-06-08):** `Rnd` (commons/utils, full API) now ported at `Commons/Utils/Rnd.cs` — canonical; sites inlining `Random.Shared` (e.g. `SpawnGroup`) can migrate later. `AtomicBoolean` also exists. `DamageList` (controllers/attack) ported (WIP — forward-refs `Creature` spine + `TeamDamageList`).
- **🔁 `TeamDamageList` (controllers/attack, 50L) blocked on the team hierarchy cone:** needs `TemporaryPlayerTeam<?>` → `GeneralTeam<P,TM>` → `AbstractTeam` + `LootGroupRules` + team packets + `PacketSendUtility` (deep F4; flat C# Player only has a `CurrentTeamId`/`PlayerGroupRuntime` shim). Also has a **Java-erasure cast** `(DamageInfo<Player>)(DamageInfo<? extends Creature>)` that C#'s reified generics reject → port via **reconstruct**: `var d = new DamageInfo<Player>(player); d.AddDamage(src.GetDamage());` (observably identical; the source DamageInfo is read-only at that point). When porting `TemporaryPlayerTeam<?>` as a Dictionary key, use the non-generic-base convention (define the team base shape during the team-cone port, not guessed here).
- **`Predicates` (utils/collections) is Player-blocked:** only `alwaysTrue()` is pure; the `Players` nested class needs `Player`/`Pet`/`PetFunctionType` (F4). Port with the team/Player cone.
- **✅ GeoService/GeoMap non-spine forward-refs resolved:** `HouseDoorState`, `AIQuestion`, `GeoDataConfig`, `GameServerError` ported (`GameEngine`/`ZoneName`/`WorldMapType` already existed).
- **🔁 SM_ATTACK_STATUS reconciliation — SCOPED (do at F4 with Player:Creature):** the existing C# `SmAttackStatusType`/`SmAttackStatusLog` **already carry Java's exact ids** (NaturalHp=3, Hp=7, HealMp=19, NaturalMp=22, UsedMp=23…, Log Regular=191 etc.) — only PascalCase names + a divergent objectId-based ctor. Java's `SM_ATTACK_STATUS` nests `TYPE`/`LOG` (SCREAMING_SNAKE, dup ids DAMAGE=7/HP=7 etc.) + Creature-based ctors that compute objectId+hp/mp% at write time (writeImpl switch: damage/used→ −value+hp%, used_mp/damage_mp→ −value+mp%, mp/heal_mp/natural_mp/absorbed_mp→ value+mp%, default→ value+hp%). **Reconciliation (byte-format UNCHANGED → golden-safe):** nest faithful `TYPE`/`LOG` (Java names+ids) in `SmAttackStatus`, store `Creature` + compute in WritePayload like Java, add the 3 Creature ctors, migrate the 5 existing objectId-based callers. **Gated on `Player:Creature`** (callers currently hold objectId not Creature). Unblocks `CreatureLifeStats` + `AttackShieldObserver`.
- **🔁 `CreatureLifeStats` is packet-reconciliation-blocked:** its public API uses Java's `SM_ATTACK_STATUS.TYPE`/`.LOG` nested enums (specific client ids: TYPE NATURAL_HP=3/REGULAR=5/HP=7/HEAL_MP=19/NATURAL_MP=22…, LOG REGULAR/MPHEAL=4…). The existing C# `SmAttackStatus` **diverged**: separate PascalCase `SmAttackStatusType`/`SmAttackStatusLog` + an objectId-based ctor `SmAttackStatus(int creatureObjectId, type, skillId, value, hpOrMpPercentage, log, usesNegativeValue?)` instead of Java's `SM_ATTACK_STATUS(Creature owner, TYPE, skillId, value, LOG)`. **Reconcile per rule 8 (Java wins):** restore faithful nested `TYPE`/`LOG` (Java ids) + `Creature`-based ctor on `SmAttackStatus`, migrate the packet-path callers, THEN port `CreatureLifeStats` referencing the faithful enums. Do this as a dedicated packet step, not a rushed forward-ref.
- **📐 geoEngine cone (C# had no geoEngine at all):** ✅ math `Vector3f`/`Vector2f`/`FastMath`; ✅ collision `Collidable`/`UnsupportedCollisionException`/`CollisionIntention`(+`CollisionIntentions`)/`IgnoreProperties`/`CollisionResult`/`CollisionResults`; ✅ `utils/TempVars` (forward-refs Matrix3f + BIHNode.BIHStackData). ✅ math `Matrix3f`(1242L). ✅ `bounding/BoundingVolume`(201L)+`BoundingBox`(616L). **Remaining:** math `Matrix4f`(1829L) (pure, big — needed by BoundingBox.Transform `MultProj`/`ToRotationMatrix`; mostly rendering API, port when collision actually needs it); `scene` ✅`CollisionData`/✅`Spatial`(257L)/✅`scene/mesh` `IndexArray`+`IndexByteArray`+`IndexShortArray` — ✅`Geometry`(167L)/✅`Node`(496L)/✅`DespawnableNode`(139L) — remaining `Mesh`(150L, needs BIHTree); ✅`collision/bih` `BIHNode`(235L)+`BIHTree`(238L). ✅`math/Ray`(373L, complete — no forward refs). ✅`scene/Mesh`(150L). **Remaining:** `models/GeoMap` (the .geo loader); `Commons.Nio` buffers: ✅`ByteOrder`+✅`Buffer`+✅ **`Commons.Nio` COMPLETE** (ByteOrder, Buffer, Byte/Float/Short buffers + Heap + AsFloat/AsShort views). Forward refs surfaced by GeoService/GeoMap: `GameEngine`(model interface), `GeoDataConfig`, `HouseDoorState`, `WorldMapType.GetWorld/ORIEL/PERNON`, `AIQuestion.CONSIDER_BOUNDS_*`, `NpcTemplate`, `DataManager.WORLD_MAPS_DATA/NPC_DATA` (statics — singleton-bridge), + the Creature/Npc/Player/VisibleObject spine (the SCC). **NOTE:** scene-cone namespace caveat — index arrays are in lowercase `...Scene.mesh` (mirrors Java `scene.mesh`) to avoid clashing with the `Mesh` class FQN in `...Scene`. **Order:** Matrix3f → Matrix4f → BoundingVolume → BoundingBox → Geometry/Mesh/Spatial/Node → BIH → Ray → GeoMap → GeoService. **Watch:** Java signed-`byte` semantics (see CollisionIntention GetId→sbyte). **Convention:** Java `protected` fields are package-accessible (cross-instance within a package); C# `protected` is NOT (CS1540 when reading another instance's protected field from a sibling/subclass). When a port needs that (e.g. `DespawnableNode.copyFrom` reading another `Node`'s fields), map Java `protected` → **C# `protected internal`** (same-assembly = same-package-ish). Applied to `Spatial.name`, `Node.collisionIntentions/materialId`. **Forward dep:** `FloatBuffer` (java.nio) — referenced by `Matrix3f.FillFloatBuffer` (and likely Matrix4f); no C# equivalent, niche rendering helper. Port a minimal faithful `FloatBuffer` (Put(float) chainable, position/limit) under `Aion.GameServer.Commons.Nio` only when something actually calls it; otherwise it stays a red-branch forward ref. **NOW a real subsystem need:** the `.geo` loader (GeoMap) + IndexArray use java.nio `Buffer`/`ByteBuffer`/`ShortBuffer`/`FloatBuffer` (Limit()/Get/Put/position, big-endian `order`). Port a faithful minimal `Commons.Nio` buffer set before `GeoMap`. Watch endianness — Java nio default is BIG-endian; .geo files have a defined byte order, so match Java's buffer order exactly (don't assume BinaryReader little-endian). geoEngine math uses **PascalCase** API (`X/Y/Z`, `Add`/`Distance`…) + `GetX`/`SetX`; namespace `Aion.GameServer.GeoEngine.Math` (use `using JMath = System.Math;` to avoid the `Math` clash); constants keep Java SCREAMING names.

Port each with its subsystem.

Only deferred member: `AttackShieldObserver` (SM_ATTACK_STATUS reconciliation). Original cone notes:
- **🔭 ObserveController cone (no packets/singletons — clean SCC entry):** ✅ bases `ActionObserver`+`AttackCalcObserver`; ✅ subclasses `AttackStatusObserver`/`AttackerCriticalStatusObserver`/`DeathObserver`/`StartMovingListener`/`DialogObserver`/`ItemUseObserver`/`StanceObserver`. **Remaining before `ObserveController` (270L):** `ShieldObserver`, `FlyRingObserver`, `RoadObserver`, `AbstractQuestZoneObserver`, `AbstractCollisionObserver`+actors (`AbstractMaterialSkillActor`/`CollisionDieActor`/`TerrainZoneCollisionMaterialActor`/`ZoneCollisionMaterialActor`), and **deferred `AttackShieldObserver`** (244L — needs SM_ATTACK_STATUS TYPE/LOG reconciliation + Effect engine; port with `CreatureLifeStats`). Then `ObserveController` itself (forward-refs `Creature`/`Player`/`Item`/`Effect`/`Skill`). `ObserverType`/`AttackerCriticalStatus`/`ShieldType` ported.
- **✅ `AggroList` ported (controllers/attack, WIP)** — first application of the singleton-bridge convention below. Still forward-refs: `Creature`/`SummonedObject`(non-generic base)/`Player` spine, `StatFunctions.CalculateHate` (→`Utils.Stats`, MISSING), `GeoService.GetInstance().CanSee` (→`World.Geo`, MISSING), `DataManager.TRIBE_RELATIONS_DATA` (static member, MISSING — C# DataManager is DI-instance), `ThreadPoolManager.GetInstance()` (static accessor, MISSING — C# is DI).
- **🏛️ SINGLETON-BRIDGE CONVERGENCE DECISION (2026-06-08, adopted per doctrine):** Java exposes engine services as **static singletons** (`ThreadPoolManager.getInstance()`, `GeoService.getInstance()`) and data as **static fields** (`DataManager.TRIBE_RELATIONS_DATA`); the C# port chose **DI**. Per-instance domain objects (`Creature`, `AggroList`, life/game stats, controllers…) can't easily receive DI and must match Java 1:1. **Resolution:** add **Java-style static accessors** — `ThreadPoolManager.GetInstance()`, `GeoService.GetInstance()`, static `DataManager.TRIBE_RELATIONS_DATA` (etc.) — **bridged to the DI container at server startup** (one assignment in composition root). Domain code stays 1:1 with Java; DI services keep working. This is the agreed convention for the whole Creature SCC; apply it as engines/`DataManager` statics are added. (User doctrine: "Java is the only truth; refactor C# to match Java better.") **✅ `ThreadPoolManager.GetInstance()`/`RegisterInstance()` implemented + wired in `Program.cs` after `host.Build()`.** Still needed: static `GeoService.GetInstance()`, static `DataManager.TRIBE_RELATIONS_DATA` (+ other statics), `StatFunctions` (static class — naturally static, no bridge needed).
- Big slop clusters (BindPointTeleport, FindGroup, WorldNpc, PlayerKnown, summons, vortex) are **substrate-blocked** — they fake unported runtime; don't attempt top-down by file count. Port substrate first.
- Golden harness covers deterministic, constructor-driven packets + pure formulas only; singleton/time-dependent packets need a deterministic config harness first.
- `check_fidelity.py` matches by simple (normalized) class name — a faithful port named differently from its Java class can trip it; fix by matching the Java name, not by editing the baseline.

## PHASE TRANSITION (leaf-porting → pillars)
The standalone clean-leaf supply is **exhausted** across all mined packages (dataholders ~95% done; quest models cone complete; configs/utils/stat-enums/account/itemgroups/siege-data done; QuestTemplate+NpcTemplate+SkillData+SkillTreeData landed). Verified this tick: commons.network packet bases (BaseClientPacket/BaseServerPacket), LoginServerConnection, StaticDataListener(JAXB Unmarshaller.Listener) all unported → everything remaining is **pillar/cone-gated**.

**Remaining unported (~1090) is dominated by convergence pillars (all RED-by-design, name-guardrail-tolerated):**
1. **network/commons packet layer** — BaseClientPacket/BaseServerPacket + ByteBuffer abstraction + connections → unblocks 433 aion packets + loginserver packets + PacketSendUtility. (biggest fan-out)
2. **JAXB→XmlSerializer bridge** (JAXBUtil + Unmarshaller.Listener + @XmlID/@XmlIDREF + StaticData async) → unblocks all dataholder Load()/NpcData/ZoneData/WalkerData/PlayerInitialData + config Load().
3. **AbstractQuestHandler (1290L god-class)** → unblocks 17 template handlers + scripted quests.
4. **skillengine/effect cone (151)**, **dao (57, DB pillar)**, **Quartz cron** (CronExpression/Job), **AIEngine/AbstractAI**, **ZoneTemplate slop re-port (task_1903432a)**, **team-event wildcard-generic cone (AbstractTeamPlayerEvent/TemporaryPlayerTeam<?>)**, **VortexLocation/AutoInstance instance cones**.
These are multi-tick efforts; recommend dedicated focused sessions (or spawned worktree tasks) per pillar rather than 60s-tick slices. Outstanding spawned tasks: task_1903432a (ZoneTemplate), task_1b3c5acb (Passport — delivered), task_3febc765 (StringFunction casing).
- **(addendum to phase transition — 2 standalone utils were actually portable): dataholders/loadingutils/adapters SpaceSeparatedBytesAdapter (Neon; Java extends XmlAdapter<String,byte[]>→plain class w/ Marshal(sbyte[])/Unmarshal(string) [no C# XmlAdapter base; consumers use [XmlIgnore]+Raw-prop idiom delegating here]; **Java signed byte→sbyte, Byte.parseByte→sbyte.Parse**) + network/sequrity NetFlusher (NB4L1; **java.util.Timer/TimerTask scheduleAtFixedRate→System.Threading.Timer per task retained in static List to prevent GC**; Runnable→Action; RuntimeException→Exception; printStackTrace→Console.Error.WriteLine) DONE — GREEN.** Remaining 0-import standalone scan items are all genuinely pillar-coupled (AIEventLog[LinkedBlockingDeque]/HpPhases[NpcAI]/Link[PlayerModel]/CurrentThreadRunnableRunner+RunnableRunner+CronExpressions[Quartz]/Actions+PeriodicActions[effect]/WalkerFormationsCache+WorldWalkerFormations[InstanceWalkerFormations cone]/StringSchemaOutputResolver+XmlValidationHandler[JAXB bridge]).

## PILLAR 1 START: commons network packet layer
- **commons/network/packet BasePacket (Aquanox; opCode + GetPacketName[getClass().getSimpleName()→GetType().Name] + ToFormattedPacketNameString[String.format %0Nd→ToString("D"+N)]; GetOpCodeZeroPadding made virtual [Java protected overridable]) DONE — GREEN** at Commons/Network/Packet/BasePacket.cs. **DISCOVERY: a java.nio ByteBuffer port ALREADY EXISTS at Commons/Nio (Buffer/ByteBuffer/HeapByteBuffer/ByteOrder/Float+ShortBuffer views) but only has Put(byte)/Put(byte[]) — MISSING PutInt/PutShort/PutChar/PutDouble/PutFloat/PutLong (+ Get* counterparts likely) needed by BaseServerPacket.writeD/H/C/DF/F/Q/S.** NEXT PILLAR INCREMENTS: (1) add ByteOrder-aware Put*/Get* primitives to Commons/Nio ByteBuffer+HeapByteBuffer (Java semantics: big-endian default, packets use LITTLE_ENDIAN via order()); (2) port BaseServerPacket (139L; buf field + writeD/H/C/DF/F/Q/S/B); (3) port BaseClientPacket (267L; read counterparts readD/H/C/F/DF/Q/S/B + Runnable); (4) LsServerPacket/LsClientPacket/LoginServerConnection; (5) AionServerPacket/AionClientPacket bases → then the 433-packet cone becomes leaf-portable.
- **PILLAR 1 increment: Commons/Nio ByteBuffer — added Put*/Get* primitives** (PutShort/PutInt/PutLong/PutChar/PutFloat/PutDouble [relative + absolute, ByteOrder-aware] + GetLong/GetDouble/GetChar) matching java.nio semantics (bigEndian default; LITTLE_ENDIAN via Order()). PutFloat→SingleToInt32Bits+PutInt; PutDouble→DoubleToInt64Bits+PutLong; PutChar→PutShort (2-byte). Uses existing Buffer.NextPutIndex/NextGetIndex + abstract Put(index,byte). **Now BaseServerPacket.writeD/H/C/DF/F/Q/S/B + BaseClientPacket.readD/H/C/F/DF/Q/S can bind.** NEXT: port commons BaseServerPacket (buf field→ByteBuffer; writeS putChar UTF-16 + '\000' terminator) then BaseClientPacket.
- **PILLAR 1 increment: commons/network/packet BaseServerPacket (-Nemesiss-; abstract : BasePacket; public ByteBuffer buf + SetBuf; WriteD/H/C(int)/C(byte)/DF/F/Q/S/B over ByteBuffer Put*) DONE — GREEN.** writeS→UTF-16 chars + '\0' terminator. **TOOLING TRAP: typing a null-char literal via Write inserts a raw 0x00 byte (file flagged "binary", Edit can't match it) — fixed by Python byte-replace 0x00→`\0` escape. (Same class of gremlin as PUA chars; author control-char literals via Python.)** NEXT: BaseClientPacket (267L; read side readD/H/C/F/DF/Q/S/B + Run/runImpl + BaseClientPacket<T> generic connection param).
- **PILLAR 1 increment: commons/utils NetworkUtils (KID,-Nemesiss-; FindLocalIPv4[java.net NetworkInterface→System.Net.NetworkInformation, InetAddress→IPAddress, multicast=first octet 224-239] + CheckIPMatching[**Byte.parseByte→sbyte.Parse, preserves Java quirk: octets >127 throw**] + IntToIpString + ToHex(ByteBuffer)/ToText[String.format %04X/%02X→ToString("X4")/("X2")]) DONE — GREEN.** Uses Commons/Nio ByteBuffer (Get(i)/Limit()/Capacity()). Unblocks BaseClientPacket's NetworkUtils.toHex logging dep. **PACKET PILLAR STATUS: write path done (BasePacket+ByteBuffer Put*/Get*+BaseServerPacket+NetworkUtils). BLOCKED on the NIO connection cone (AConnection<T extends BaseServerPacket>/AionConnection/Crypt/Dispatcher/Acceptor/IOServer — large subsystem) which gates BaseClientPacket[`T extends AConnection<?>` wildcard bound] + AionServerPacket[writeImpl(AionConnection)] + all 433 packets. The connection/NIO layer is the next big pillar — recommend dedicated effort.**
- **PILLAR 1 increment: Commons/Nio ByteBuffer.Array()/ArrayOffset() (java.nio backing-array accessors) + network/EncryptionKeyPair (cura; Aion packet cipher, 188L) DONE — GREEN.** EncryptionKeyPair: staticKey="nKO/...?3iI9"[64 chars, i&63]→Encoding.ASCII.GetBytes; **Java signed byte[]→C# byte[] (unsigned; all key/data math &0xff → bit-identical); byte^=int→byte=(byte)(byte^int); System.arraycopy→Array.Copy; currentTimeMillis()→ToUnixTimeMilliseconds; Integer.toHexString→ToString("x"); Decrypt/Encrypt mutate buf.Array() in place at ArrayOffset()+Position(); ValidateClientPacket getShort(0)==~getShort(3) [short→int promotion] && get(2)==staticClientPacketCode**. Self-contained (only ByteBuffer dep). **NEXT: Crypt (92L; delegates to EncryptionKeyPair[now green]; enableKey Rnd.nextInt^0xCD92E4DF+0x3FF2CCCF; encode/decodeOpcode use SM_VERSION_CHECK.INTERNAL_VERSION[red-tolerated]).**
- **PILLAR 1 increment: network/Crypt (hack99,kao,-Nemesiss-; delegates to EncryptionKeyPair[green]) DONE — GREEN.** EnableKey: Rnd.NextInt()→new EncryptionKeyPair; **return (key ^ unchecked((int)0xCD92E4DF)) + 0x3FF2CCCF wrapped in unchecked() [Java int-hex-literal wraps; C# 0xCD92E4DF is uint so cast]**; IllegalStateException→InvalidOperationException; Encrypt skips first packet (SM_KEY); EncodeServerPacketOpcode/DecodeClientPacketOpcode (precedence: `-` before `^`, parens preserved) use SM_VERSION_CHECK.INTERNAL_VERSION[red-tolerated]. staticServerPacketCode=0x44. **PACKET PILLAR write+crypt foundation COMPLETE: BasePacket/ByteBuffer(Put*/Get*/Array)/BaseServerPacket/NetworkUtils/EncryptionKeyPair/Crypt all green. Still gated: AConnection/AionConnection NIO layer (java.nio.channels Selector/SocketChannel — needs Socket-based rewrite, not 1:1) + AionServerPacket(writeImpl(AionConnection)) + BaseClientPacket(T extends AConnection<?>). The NIO channel/selector layer is the remaining hard pillar.**

## AUTONOMOUS-PHASE BOUNDARY (foundation done → big pillars remain)
Clean leaves AND the packet write/crypt foundation are now ported. The remaining ~1085 unported files are large coupled units that are **not well-suited to 60s autonomous slices** — each needs focused, reviewable work:
1. **NIO connection/dispatcher layer** (commons.network AConnection<T extends BaseServerPacket>/Dispatcher/Acceptor/IOServer + AionConnection): deeply tied to java.nio.channels SocketChannel/Selector/SelectionKey — this is a **designed Socket/async rewrite, NOT a 1:1 line port**. Gates AionServerPacket(writeImpl(AionConnection)) + capture observers + BaseClientPacket(T extends AConnection<?> wildcard) + all 433 packets. ⇒ dedicated architectural session.
2. **AbstractQuestHandler (1290L)** — large but genuinely 1:1 (dispatch logic, red deps tolerated). ⇒ SPAWNED task_3f5565d9.

## 2026-06-10 — world/zone/ZoneLevelService (+ ThreadPool idiom fix)
Ported ZoneLevelService (drowning/death-level checks). **Discovered the C# ThreadPoolManager is a reworked ASYNC API** (NOT a 1:1 of Java): Schedule/ScheduleAtFixedRateTask take Func<CancellationToken, ValueTask> + TimeSpan, returning ScheduledTask. Established ecosystem idiom (used by AbstractMaterialSkillActor, PlayerController, etc.): `Schedule(ct => { ...; return ValueTask.CompletedTask; }, TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(N))` and `ScheduleAtFixedRateTask(ct => {...; return ValueTask.CompletedTask;}, TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(init), TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(period))`. Java anon Runnable -> that lambda. TaskId.DROWN -> TaskId.Drown (PascalCase; note existing slop PlayerController.cs uses wrong TaskId.PROTECTION_ACTIVE). Math.round(maxHp/10f) -> (int)Math.Floor(+0.5f). ReduceHp(SM_ATTACK_STATUS.TYPE.DROWNING,...) returns int ==0. **Also fixed my prior WorldMapInstance tick**: its Schedule(()=>{...},1500) call now uses the correct ct/ValueTask/TimeSpan idiom. World.GetInstance/GetWorldMap red-tolerated (World = partial god class). NUL-clean, guardrail green.

Deleted all 38 (git, reversible); guardrail reported "34 slop files removed"; ratcheted baseline 363->329. **Remediation workflow: per exploded Java service, confirm the faithful 1:1 C# port exists+covers behavior, verify fragments have no external refs, git rm the fragment cluster, --update-baseline.** Next clusters: many more *PlanService explosions (AbyssAnnouncement/AdventCalendar/ApExtraction/Arcade/etc.). ## 2026-06-11 — SLOP REMEDIATION: 122 truly-dead files (-122, baseline 329->207)
After the BindPointTeleport cluster, scripted a safe batch detector (memory [[slop-remediation-workflow]]): for each banned-vocab slop file, grep -rlw the class name; if referenced by NO other .cs file -> truly dead orphan (invented abstraction wired into nothing) -> 100% safe to delete. Found 122/329 dead; git rm'd all (reversible); ratcheted baseline 329->207. **Process pitfall: Python open('w') on Windows writes CRLF -> stray \r corrupts paths in `$(cat file)`; fix with `tr -d '\r'` or newline='\n'.** Remaining 207 slop files ARE referenced (by reworked stack like GameClientSocketServer/PlayerAllianceRuntime, or by other slop) — those need caller-redirect-to-faithful-service before deletion (NOT blind-deletable). FindGroup (103 files!) is the largest woven cluster. NUL-clean, guardrail green.

## 2026-06-11 — DAO PILLAR opens: dao/MotionDAO (plateau was WRONG — 57 DAOs are faithful-portable)
Re-audited "missing basenames" (comm java-vs-cs): 126 total = **57 DAOs** + 35 CM_/SM_ bridge packets + 34 deferred-infra/false-positives. **The 57 dao/*DAO.java were wrongly lumped into "deferred infra" — they are faithful-portable additive units.** The current aion-server DAO architecture is NOT the old abstract-DAO+DAOManager: each dao/FooDAO.java is a plain class with static JDBC methods over DatabaseFactory.getConnection(). The working C# build reimplements persistence under a DIFFERENT abstraction (Data/*Repository.cs, async interface+Empty+MySql triple) — e.g. MotionRepository.cs implements only 1 of MotionDAO's 4 methods, so the faithful 1:1 DAO is the more-complete literal truth. C# has NO dao/ dir, so porting DAOs is fully ADDITIVE (no name collision, zero risk to working build), red-tolerated.
**DAO idiom established (reusable for remaining 56):** namespace Aion.GameServer.Dao; class keeps `DAO` suffix; static methods PascalCase; SQL query constants `public const string` preserved VERBATIM (backticks/table/column names — DB backward-compat). JDBC try-with-resources -> `using MySqlConnection con = DatabaseFactory.GetConnection(); con.Open(); using MySqlCommand stmt = con.CreateCommand(); stmt.CommandText = Q;`. Positional `?` PreparedStatement params -> ordered unnamed `stmt.Parameters.Add(new MySqlParameter { Value = x });` (matches the `?` placeholders, same idiom as the reworked Repositories). rset.getInt/getBoolean("col") -> `reader.GetInt32(reader.GetOrdinal("col"))`/`GetBoolean(...)`. stmt.executeQuery()->ExecuteReader()+while(Read()); stmt.execute()->ExecuteNonQuery(). Logger via NullLoggerFactory; Java's PlayerEmotionListDAO.class copy-paste logger-category quirk preserved literally. Synchronous (mirrors Java DAO). DatabaseFactory in Aion.Commons.Database; MySqlConnector already referenced by the project.
Deps red-tolerated as usual (Player.SetMotions etc.). **Next: continue the DAO pillar — BlockListDAO, PlayerPasskeyDAO, and the other 54 (AbyssRankDAO/PlayerDAO/InventoryDAO/LegionDAO/MailDAO/...).** NUL-clean, guardrail green (baseline 126 unchanged — DAOs aren't slop).

## 2026-06-11 — Compile-convergence tick 7: SkillEngine namespace-casing convergence (2360 -> 1958, -402)
Targeted EffectTemplate(79)/Skill(35) CS0246 — both types EXIST; the real root was a pervasive **namespace case-split**: Java lowercase packages (`skillengine`, `skillengine.change/condition/action/periodicaction`, `geoengine.scene.mesh`) were transcribed into C# inconsistently as BOTH `SkillEngine.*` and `Skillengine.*` (and lowercase sub-segments), and C# namespaces are case-sensitive so the halves never unified — files importing `Skillengine.Effects`/`SkillEngine.change` couldn't see types declared under the other casing.
Fix chain (all faithful; Java type names preserved; casing/qualification = authorized Java->C# collision divergences):

**EXACT java.nio.channels SHIM SPEC (next tick — port into Aion.Commons.Nio.Channels over System.Net.Sockets; signatures from reading AConnection/Dispatcher/Acceptor/NioServer):**
- `SelectionKey`: const int OP_READ=1,OP_WRITE=4,OP_CONNECT=8,OP_ACCEPT=16; int InterestOps()/InterestOps(int); int ReadyOps(); bool IsValid()/IsReadable()/IsWritable()/IsAcceptable(); SelectableChannel Channel(); object Attachment()/Attach(object); void Cancel(); Selector Selector().
- `SelectableChannel` (abstract): SelectionKey Register(Selector,int ops,object att); SelectableChannel ConfigureBlocking(bool); SelectionKey KeyFor(Selector).
- `SocketChannel : SelectableChannel`: static Open(); int Read(ByteBuffer); int Write(ByteBuffer); void Close(); Socket-facade Socket() exposing GetInetAddress().GetHostAddress() (-> RemoteEndPoint IP). Wraps System.Net.Sockets.Socket.
- `ServerSocketChannel : SelectableChannel`: static Open(); facade Socket().Bind(EndPoint); SocketChannel Accept().
- `Selector`: static (via SelectorProvider.Provider().OpenSelector()); int Select()/SelectNow(); ISet<SelectionKey> SelectedKeys(); ISet<SelectionKey> Keys(); Selector Wakeup(); void Close(). Impl over Socket.Select(read,write,error,timeout) across registered channels.
- `SelectorProvider`: static Provider(); Selector OpenSelector().
- ByteBuffer (Aion.Commons.Nio, exists) needs backing-array access for Socket.Receive/Send into the buffer at position..limit; verify/extend ByteBuffer API.
